Keep\nthe scalar body and add a kernel in front of it:\n\n```cpp\n#include \"../dsp/simd.hpp\"\n\nnamespace kernels {\n#if CEDAR_SIMD_X86\nCEDAR_TARGET_AVX2 inline void mul_avx2(const float* a, const float* b, float* out) noexcept {\n    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < BLOCK_SIZE; i += 8)\n        _mm256_store_ps(out + i, _mm256_mul_ps(_mm256_load_ps(a + i), _mm256_load_ps(b + i)));\n}\n#endif\n}\n\ninline void op_mul(ExecutionContext& ctx, const Instruction& inst) {\n    // ... resolve a, b, out ...\n#if CEDAR_SIMD_X86\n    if (simd::has(simd::Isa::Avx2)) { kernels::mul_avx2(a, b, out); return; }\n#endif\n    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < BLOCK_SIZE; ++i) out[i] = a[i] * b[i];  // fallback stays\n}\n```\n\n- `CEDAR_TARGET_AVX2` is `[[gnu::target(\"avx2\")]]`; no compiler flags change.\n  FMA is **not** enabled (it would change rounding).\n- Buffers from `ctx.buffers->get()` are 32-byte aligned; `BLOCK_SIZE` is 128.\n- You may also add NEON (`CEDAR_SIMD_NEON`) or WASM SIMD128\n  (`CEDAR_SIMD_WASM`) paths; they are measured later on other machines, not\n  gated here.\n\n## Rules (enforced by the verifier \u2014 violations are rejected automatically)\n\n1. You may only edit: `cedar/include/cedar/opcodes/**`, `cedar/include/cedar/dsp/simd*.hpp`, `cedar/src/dsp/simd*.cpp`. Any other edit (benchmarks, tests,\n   experiments, CMake, scripts) rejects the whole attempt.\n2. **Never delete or change the scalar body.** It is the oracle and the\n   fallback; the verifier runs it with SIMD disabled and requires\n   bit-identical output to the original.\n3. Output must be **bit-identical** to the original for every opcode.\n   No approximations are allowed for these opcodes.\n4. The final verification also runs every opcode on **randomised stimuli you\n   cannot see** (different signals, levels down to denormals, parameter\n   trajectories). A kernel that only works for the stimuli in the benchmark\n   source fails there.\n5. No allocation, locks or syscalls in a kernel. CPU detection is already done\n   once at start-up; never detect features per block or per sample.\n6. The change must make at least one listed opcode **\u2265 5 % faster** than the\n   *current* version (outside measurement noise) and none slower.\n\n## Budget\n\nAt most **10 runs of the check command** and **30 minutes** for\nthis attempt. Every model in this experiment gets the same budget.\n\nYour tools are: read, write and edit files, search the repository, and run\nthe check command. **The check command is the only shell command allowed**\n(piping its output, e.g. through `tail`, is fine).    // stimulus buffers 0..4\n    46\tconstexpr std::uint16_t OUT_BASE = 8;   // outputs at 8 + 2k (stereo pairs)\n    47\tconstexpr float SR = 48000.0f;\n    48\tconstexpr std::uint16_t NONE = 0xFFFF;\n    49\t\n    50\t// Stimulus for one input slot at absolute sample index n (0..STIM_BLOCKS*BLOCK_SIZE).\n    51\tusing Stim = std::function<float(std::size_t n)>;\n    52\t\n    53\t// --seed N (N != 0) switches every stimulus to a randomised \"hidden\" variant\n    54\t// (PRD \u00a76 / research P5): the full verify picks a fresh seed per call and\n    55\t// dumps baseline and candidate with it, so a kernel cannot be tuned to the\n    56\t// fixed stimuli the quick check and the timing path use.\n    57\tstd::uint32_t g_seed = 0;\n    58\tstd::mt19937 g_hidden_rng; Every seed is guaranteed one\n    78\t    // near-denormal noise segment (1e-35..1e-25) and one hot one (2-4x),\n    79\t    // at random positions, so that coverage never depends on luck.\n    80\t    void make_hidden(std::mt19937& rng) {\n    81\t        std::uniform_real_distribution<float> u(0.0f, 1.0f);\n    82\t        constexpr std::size_t seg = STIM_BLOCKS * BLOCK_SIZE / 8;\n    83\t        const auto tiny_seg = static_cast<std::size_t>(u(rng) * 8.0f) % 8;\n    84\t        const auto hot_seg = (tiny_seg + 1 + static_cast<std::size_t>(u(rng) * 7.0f) % 7) % 8;\n    85\t        for (std::size_t s = 0; s < 8; ++s) {\n    86\t            int kind = static_cast<int>(u(rng) * 4.0f);\n    87\t            float level = gain * std::pow(10.0f, -30.0f * u(rng) * u(rng)) * (1.0f + 3.0f * u(rng));\n    88\t            if (s == tiny_seg) { kind = 0; level = gain * std::pow(10.0f, -25.0f - 10.0f * u(rng)); }\n    89\t            if (s == hot_seg) { kind = 0; level = gain * (2.0f + 2.0f * u(rng)); }\n    90\t            float dc = level * (2.0f * u(rng) - 1.0f);\n    91\t            for (std::size_t k = s * seg; k < (s + 1) * seg; ++k) {\n    92\t                switch (kind) {\n    93\t                    case 0: noise[k] *= level; break;\n    94\t                    case 1: noise[k] = u(rng) < 0.002f ? level * (u(rng) < 0.5f ? -1.0f : 1.0f) : 0.0f; break;\n    95\t                    case 2: noise[k] = (k % 4096 == 0) ? (dc = level * (2.0f * u(rng) - 1.0f)) : dc; break;\n    96\t                    default: noise[k] = 0.0f; break;\n    97\t                }\n    98\t            }\n    99\t        }\n   100\t    }\n   101\t    float operator()(std::size_t n) const {\n   102\t        if (g_seed) return noise[n];\n   103\t        constexpr std::size_t total = STIM_BLOCKS * BLOCK_SIZE;\n   104\t        constexpr std::size_t quarter = total / 4;\n   105\t        std::size_t seg = n / quarter, k = n % quarter;\n   106\t        float t = static_cast<float>(k) / static_cast<float>(quarter);\n   107\t        switch (seg) {\n   108\t            case 0: return gain * noise[n];\n   109\t            case 1: {\n   110\t                // 20 Hz \u2192 20 kHz exponential sweep, phase-integrated\n   111\t                double f0 = 20.0, f1 = 20000.0, T = double(quarter) / double(SR);\n   112\t                double tt = double(k) / double(SR);\n   113\t                double kk = std::log(f1 / f0) / T;\n   114\t                double ph = 2.0 * 3.14159265358979 * f0 * (std::exp(kk * tt) - 1.0) / kk;\n   115\t                return gain * static_cast<float>(std::sin(ph));\n   116\t            }\n   117\t            case 2: return (k % 997 == 0) ? gain : 0.0f;\n   118\t            default: return gain * (2.0f * t - 1.0f);\n   119\t        }\n   120\t    }\n   121\t};\n   122\t\n   123\tStim ramp(float lo, float hi, std::size_t period) {\n   124\t    if (g_seed) {  // hidden: same range, random rate and phase\n   125\t        std::uniform_real_distribution<float> u(0.0f, 1.0f);\n   126\t        auto p = static_cast<std::size_t>(static_cast<float>(period) * (0.05f + 3.0f * u(g_hidden_rng))) + 1;\n   127\t        auto off = static_cast<std::size_t>(u(g_hidden_rng) * static_cast<float>(p));\n   128\t        return [=](std::size_t n) { return lo + (hi - lo) * frac(n + off, p); };\n   129\t    }\n   130\t    return [=](std::size_t n) { return lo + (hi - lo) * frac(n, period); };\n   131\t}\n   132\tStim constant(float v) { return [=](std::size_t) { return v; }; }\n   133\t\n   134\tstruct Case {\n   135\t    const char* name;\n   136\t    Opcode op;\n   137\t    std::vector<Stim> inputs; Commit K (followup, `d95aa8b`): `VM::execute_step` unifies subprogram-body dispatch so BLOCK_CALL / BLOCK_BIND / SKIP_IF / LOOP_STATIC dispatch from inside every body context, not just `execute_program`'s main loop.\ndocs/prd-runtime-functions-control-flow.md:4: > - **L2 (Phase 2) \u2014 SHIPPED.** Shipped first: the `#inline` annotation (lexer `#` token + statement-position parsing) and recursion rejection (E240/E244/E246/E249). Shipped completing L2: eligible non-`#inline` user `fn`s called from \u22652 sites compile **once** into a subprogram block and dispatch at runtime via a real `BLOCK_CALL` opcode \u2014 body stored once in `ProgramSlot::instructions[]`, relieving program-size / i-cache pressure. **Design pivot from \u00a74.2 \u2014 the swap-time `expand_block_calls()` model was abandoned.** Compile-time expansion produces audio bytecode byte-identical to per-site inlining (zero size/i-cache benefit \u2014 see \u00a76.1); instead L2 reuses L3's already-shipped runtime subprogram table (`ProgramSlot::blocks[]`, the block-aware load handshake, `set_state_id_xor` isolation). `BLOCK_CALL` carries `block_id` in `inst.rate`, caller args in `inst.inputs[0..4]`, result in `inst.out_buffer`, a per-call-site disambiguator in `inst.state_id`; `BlockEntry` gained `param_base` + `body_output` (12 bytes). `RET` stays a reserved no-op \u2014 body length lives in `BlockEntry::length`. Eligibility = speculative-compile-with-rollback (cheap signature gate, then a one-shot body compile rolled back on any error / empty-or-oversize body / nested dispatch opcode); ineligible or non-dispatchable call sites (string/array/record/`_` args) fall back to inlining \u2014 a pure optimization. Per-call-site state isolation + hot-swap continuity via a `block:<fn>@callsite_<N>` path hash XOR-composed by the VM. Rollback knob: the `inline_all_fns` compiler option forces the pre-L2 inline-everywhere behavior. **`BLOCK_BIND` (\u00a74.2) shipped as a follow-up (2026-05-20):** the >5-param slot path is now built \u2014 `fn`s with 6..`MAX_SHARED_FN_PARAMS` (32) parameters are shareable. Params 0..4 ride `BLOCK_CALL.inputs[0..4]`; params 5..N-1 ride a contiguous run of `BLOCK_BIND` opcodes (slot index in `rate`, source buffer in `inputs[0]`) emitted immediately before the `BLOCK_CALL`. The VM dispatch loop scans the run and copies the extra slots into the body's convention param bank. `fn`s beyond 32 params still fall back to inlining (graceful, never an error). **Dispatch-uniformity followup (2026-05-24, `d95aa8b`):** the L2 ship wired `BLOCK_CALL` / `BLOCK_BIND` / `SKIP_IF_*` / `LOOP_STATIC` into `execute_program`'s main IP loop but left every subprogram-body runner (`run_voice_pool`, `run_foreach_per_iteration`, `run_foreach_shared`, `run_event_map_closure`, `run_event_filter_closure`, `execute_block_call`'s own body loop) driving instructions through `VM::execute()` directly \u2014 whose switch has no case for the meta-opcodes, so any `BLOCK_CALL` inside a subprogram body silently no-op'd. A new `VM::execute_step(slot, body, ip) -> next_ip` helper owns the meta-opcode dispatch and is now the single entry point for both the main loop and every body runner; nested `BLOCK_CALL` (fn-in-fn, fn-in-event-map-closure, fn-in-voice-body) now dispatches correctly."

The within-run p10/p90 spread is\n    47\t    # reported too, but it measures interrupt tails, not median noise.\n    48\t    band = (max(bmeds) - min(bmeds)) / bmed\n    49\t    spread = max((r[\"ns_p90\"] - r[\"ns_p10\"]) / r[\"ns_per_block_median\"] for r in base)\n    50\t    res = {\"opcode\": opcode, \"baseline_ns\": round(bmed, 2), \"noise_band\": round(band, 4),\n    51\t           \"p10_p90_spread\": round(spread, 4),\n    52\t           \"isa_baseline\": base[0][\"isa\"], \"governor\": base[0][\"governor\"], \"cpu\": base[0][\"cpu\"]}\n    53\t    unstable = []\n    54\t    if res[\"governor\"] != \"performance\":\n    55\t        unstable.append(f\"governor={res['governor']}\")\n    56\t    pinned = json.loads(PINNED.read_text()).get(\"opcodes\", {}) \\\n    57\t        if check_pinned and PINNED.exists() else {}  # pins are x86 gate-machine numbers\n    58\t    if opcode in pinned:\n    59\t        drift = abs(bmed - pinned[opcode]) / pinned[opcode]\n    60\t        res[\"baseline_drift\"] = round(drift, 4)\n    61\t        if drift > DRIFT_LIMIT:\n    62\t            unstable.append(f\"baseline drift {drift:.1%} vs pinned\")\n    63\t    if candidate:\n    64\t        cmed = statistics.median(r[\"ns_per_block_median\"] for r in cand)\n    65\t        speedup = bmed / cmed\n    66\t        res.update(candidate_ns=round(cmed, 2), speedup=round(speedup, 3),\n    67\t                   isa_candidate=cand[0][\"isa\"],\n    68\t                   faster=speedup >= MIN_SPEEDUP and (1 - cmed / bmed) > band)\n    69\t    res[\"unstable\"] = unstable or False\n    70\t    return res\n    71\t\n    72\t\n    73\tdef main():\n    74\t    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()\n    75\t    ap.add_argument(\"--opcode\")\n    76\t    ap.add_argument(\"--baseline\", default=\"build/release/bin/cedar_bench\")\n    77\t    ap.add_argument(\"--candidate\")\n    78\t    ap.add_argument(\"--rounds\", type=int, default=5)\n    79\t    ap.add_argument(\"--reps\", type=int, default=500)\n    80\t    ap.add_argument(\"--pin\", action=\"store_true\", help=\"re-record baselines.json for every opcode\")\n    81\t    a = ap.parse_args()\n    82\t\n    83\t    if a.pin:\n    84\t        names = subprocess.run([a.baseline, \"--list\"], check=True, capture_output=True,\n    85\t                               text=True).stdout.split()\n    86\t        rows = {n: measure(n, a.baseline, rounds=a.rounds, reps=a.reps) for n in names}\n    87\t        first = next(iter(rows.values()))\n    88\t        PINNED.write_text(json.dumps({\n    89\t            \"cpu\": first[\"cpu\"], \"governor\": first[\"governor\"], \"pinned_cpu\": CPU,\n    90\t            \"opcodes\": {n: r[\"baseline_ns\"] for n, r in rows.items()},\n    91\t            \"noise_band\": {n: r[\"noise_band\"] for n, r in rows.items()},\n    92\t        }, indent=2) + \"\\n\")\n    93\t        for n, r in sorted(rows.items(), key=lambda kv: -kv[1][\"baseline_ns\"]):\n    94\t            print(f\"{n:22s} {r['baseline_ns']:10.2f} ns/block  band {r['noise_band']:.1%}\")\n    95\t        return\n    96\t    if not a.opcode:\n    97\t        ap.error(\"--opcode required\")\n    98\t    print(json.dumps(measure(a.opcode, a.baseline, a.candidate, a.rounds, a.reps)))\n    99\t\n   100\t\n   101\tif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n   102\t    sys.exit(main())"
